### Clinic creation self-service Phase 1 — Add Clinic (SPEC-CLI-001 §10)

CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT. Migration applied to DEVELOPMENT by the user and the Android workflow physically validated there (see "DEVELOPMENT closure" below). Production has not been deployed or touched.

- Why: after Clinic self-service Phase 1 and Staff Phase 1, creating a Clinic was the only routine SQL-only step for an existing Organization to operate and grow (including its first Clinic after trusted onboarding). Clinic creation is operational self-service authorized by `ORGANIZATION_CREATE_CLINICS`; it requires no Subscription or Entitlement and is not governed by ADR-009, which still governs Organization + Owner + initial Subscription establishment. No Clinic-count or plan limit exists.
- Spec: SPEC-CLI-001 addendum §10 (BR-12–BR-18, Verification Notes 11–20) — the client-safe idempotent operation, evaluation order, creation key, what creation produces, no deletion/archive, mobile entry and interaction. RELATIONAL_MODEL `clinics` row and Create Clinic operation updated.
- Migration `20261030090000_idempotent_clinic_creation.sql`:
  - `clinics.creation_idempotency_key uuid` (nullable for earlier rows and direct trusted fixtures) with `unique (organization_id, creation_idempotency_key)`; recorded in the same insert as the Clinic, so neither exists without the other; immutable (added to `enforce_clinic_immutability`); never returned by any read; clients have no table privileges on `clinics`.
  - `create_clinic(p_organization_id uuid, p_idempotency_key uuid) returns jsonb` `{ clinic_id, organization_id, outcome: CREATED | ALREADY_CREATED }` — `authenticated` only (no `anon`, `public`, `service_role`), `SECURITY DEFINER`, empty `search_path`. Order: authentication + coherent domain User (`28000`) → `ORGANIZATION_CREATE_CLINICS` before any Organization fact (unknown and inaccessible Organizations both `42501`; nothing locked for that caller) → Organization row `FOR UPDATE`, capability re-checked → key required (`22023`) → lookup by (Organization, key) → insert one unpublished Clinic with no configuration rows. Same Organization + key returns the same Clinic for any currently authorized caller; the same key in another Organization is a separate intent and never returns or reveals the first Organization's Clinic.
  - The non-idempotent `create_clinic(uuid)` is dropped (it also disclosed unknown Organizations with `23503`). Its only callers — rollback-only suites, `run_temporal_authority_faults.py`, and `production_validation_data.sql` — now use the new form.
- Mobile (JS only; no new dependency — keys come from `uuid.v4()` of the already-installed `expo-modules-core`, backed by `java.util.UUID.randomUUID()` on Android):
  - Professional landing: the Professional Clinics entry appears when the selected Organization has Professional Clinics **or** advisory Organization-scoped `ORGANIZATION_CREATE_CLINICS` (matched by Organization id), so an Organization with zero Clinics reaches the Clinics screen.
  - Professional Clinics: Add Clinic only with that capability for the selected Organization (context read alongside Clinics; an unreadable context offers nothing). No Clinics → Organization name, calm empty state ("stays private until set up and published") and Add Clinic as primary action; with Clinics → unchanged list plus a secondary "Añadir clínica / Add clinic" pill.
  - One confirmation (private Clinic; Guardians won't see it until published; setup continues in Clinic settings) → one intent (Organization + one new key) → `create_clinic` → on authoritative success `router.push` to the new Clinic's settings overview, whose existing readiness/Missing states are the setup checklist. Push (not replace) because creation is an action on the Clinics list, not a separate screen: Back returns to a fresh Clinics read, never to a resubmission.
  - Intent store (in memory, app session only): recorded before sending, cleared on a definite answer. An uncertain answer keeps it; the screen then shows a calm notice and "Reintentar / Try again" with the same key, and offers no new Add until it resolves — also after leaving and re-entering. A definite `42501`/`28000`/`22023` ends the intent with calm copy. A pending intent always targets its own Organization; a changed selection cannot redirect it. No placeholder Clinic is ever shown. Creation is announced for accessibility; the Alert closes before navigation.
- Not in scope (unchanged): Opening Hours and Service Offerings editors; Clinic deletion/archive (an unwanted Clinic stays unpublished); Organization onboarding and billing.
- Name at creation: deliberately not required. Add Clinic creates an empty, unpublished Clinic in one atomic, idempotent write; the existing Public Profile editor sets the name afterwards, and a nameless Clinic cannot be published (SPEC-CLI-003 BR-15). No wizard; creation is not coupled to `set_clinic_public_profile`.
- Automated validation (LOCAL, clean reset; authoritative): 35/35 SQL suites PASS with zero retained rows; 4/4 real two-session races PASS (same key / two administrators, different keys / one Organization, same key / two Organizations without waiting, same key after a rolled-back first request), no deadlock; 20/20 meaningful SQL mutants caught (`run_clinic_creation_faults.py`); regression faults green — temporal 14/14 + 5/5 (including the updated `create_clinic` mutant), Staff 19/19, B1 31/31, B2 29/29, Slice C 19/19, Edge 15/15, all existing race runners, 9 harness unit tests; mobile Jest 92 suites / 1,956 tests PASS; 14/14 ad hoc mobile mutants caught; typecheck, strict unused-locals/parameters, ESLint, Prettier PASS; `git diff --check` clean.
- Rollout performed by the user: applied `20261030090000_idempotent_clinic_creation.sql` to DEVELOPMENT and loaded the mobile JS through Metro on the existing Android development build. No Edge Function, secret, native dependency or Android build.
- DEVELOPMENT closure (user-confirmed; physical Android):
  - backend ACL / signature / idempotency-constraint checks passed in DEVELOPMENT;
  - the Professional Clinics entry is offered and opens the Clinics screen;
  - the Add Clinic confirmation is clear; Cancel creates nothing; confirming creates exactly one Clinic;
  - the app lands in the new Clinic's settings, and Back navigation behaves correctly;
  - the new Clinic appears in the correct Organization only;
  - reload / re-entry creates no duplicate;
  - the existing Clinic settings flow works on the new Clinic;
  - no payment or Entitlement step is involved in creation;
  - the only finding was Spanish terminology in the confirmation ("tutores"), corrected to the established Guardian term: "La clínica se creará como privada y no será visible para los responsables de mascotas hasta que la publiques. …" (EN: "The clinic will be created as private and won't be visible to pet guardians until you publish it. …"). Copy-only change; the route test pins the new wording.
  - Not physically tested: the uncertain-response / lost-response retry path. The automated SQL races, the SQL suite and the mobile route tests remain authoritative for it.
- Non-blocking future UX idea (not implemented): after creation, Clinic settings could visually emphasize Public Profile / Clinic name as the natural first setup step.

#### Operational self-service milestone

With Add Clinic closed in DEVELOPMENT, an already-existing Organization in the appropriate trusted Subscription state can perform its routine Clinic lifecycle from the app without SQL or operator intervention: create Clinics; configure them (public profile, Location, scheduling timezone); publish and unpublish; manage Appointment availability (standard duration, weekly hours, exceptions); and manage its Staff lifecycle (invite, revoke, accept, end access).

This is operational self-service only, in DEVELOPMENT. Still trusted or follow-up:

- Organization + initial Owner + initial Subscription establishment remains trusted Commercial Onboarding (ADR-009); commercial onboarding self-service and billing are not implemented;
- Subscription state transitions remain trusted operator actions (Phase 1 policy and runbook: see "Subscription Operational Safety — Phase 1" below);
- follow-ups: Opening Hours and Service Offerings editors, post-acceptance Responsibility editing, the Veterinarian workforce (Professional Profile, VETERINARIAN invitation, Clinic Assignment, Availability);
- Production has not been deployed or touched.

### Subscription Operational Safety — Phase 1 (ADR-006 amended, SPEC-SUB-001 §14, SPEC-SUB-002 §5/§14/§15, SPEC-SUB-003 §4/§15, SPEC-SUB-004 BR-20/§14, SPEC-MED-004 BR-33, SPEC-ORG-007 BR-7)

**CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT.** Implemented and validated LOCALLY, migration applied to DEVELOPMENT by the user, and the key Subscription lifecycle physically validated on Android there (see "DEVELOPMENT closure" below). **Production has not been deployed or touched.**

- Approved Phase 1 policy now encoded (documents first, then SQL):
  - an ordinary manually managed `ACTIVE` Subscription is open-ended (no `valid_until`) and stays active until an explicit trusted transition; finite validity remains representable where a real end is known;
  - no periodic renewal operation in manual Phase 1: no `ACTIVE → ACTIVE`, no renewal faked through `PAST_DUE`, immutable establishment facts never mutated;
  - explicit transition graph: `PENDING → ACTIVE | EXPIRED`; `ACTIVE → PAST_DUE | CANCELLED | EXPIRED`; `PAST_DUE → ACTIVE | EXPIRED`; `CANCELLED → EXPIRED`; everything else (same-state, `PENDING → CANCELLED`, `PENDING → PAST_DUE` included) rejected; `PENDING → EXPIRED` permanently closes a relationship that never became active;
  - a Subscription is established only as `PENDING` or `ACTIVE`, with an initial effective time not later than the establishment statement instant (exact instant valid); validity is never forced and no end is synthesized;
  - `EXPIRED` is terminal for that period; restarting service is a new Subscription period;
  - no future-dated transitions (`effective_at <= statement_timestamp()`, still strictly later than the current state's effective time); end-of-period cancellation is `CANCELLED` + explicit `valid_until`;
  - `PAST_DUE`: no grace, no Entitlements, no automatic expiry or recovery; resolved explicitly by the operator;
  - each transition carries its own validity; nothing is inherited;
  - published Clinics stay published and discoverable after Entitlement loss; no paid-publication gate; Guardian empty availability stays deliberately reason-agnostic (`SPEC-APT-014` BR-4/BR-5 preserved, no new public signal) and only a denied request says the Clinic is not accepting online appointment requests;
  - Subscription status is visible only to authorized Organization management (`ORGANIZATION_MANAGE_SUBSCRIPTION`) through a dedicated read; `get_my_professional_context` still excludes commercial dates/provider data;
  - Clinical Amendments to existing finalized history remain possible after Entitlement loss for an otherwise authorized current Veterinarian; new Encounters do not.
- Migration `20261031090000_subscription_operational_safety.sql` (no table, column, constraint, or data change):
  - private `subscription_transition_is_allowed(text, text)` (no role may execute it) holding the graph, including `PENDING → EXPIRED`;
  - `establish_organization_subscription` (same signature/result/ACL, `service_role` only, provider-neutral references and immutable facts unchanged): after the existing exact-replay recognition, an initial state other than `PENDING`/`ACTIVE` is `23514` HINT `INITIAL_STATE_NOT_ALLOWED`, and an initial effective time later than `statement_timestamp()` is `23514` HINT `FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_AT`; exact replays still return the same Subscription and contradictory key reuse is still `23505`. It is the only insert path (no client table privilege); no insert trigger was added, so pre-existing rollback-only fixtures that load other initial states directly as data are unaffected;
  - `apply_subscription_state_transition` (same signature/result/ACL, `service_role` only): exact replays still recognized first (source event key, or semantic); then `EXPIRED` terminal → future `effective_at` → stale/equal `effective_at` → same-state → graph; every policy rejection is `23514` with HINT `SUBSCRIPTION_EXPIRED_TERMINAL` / `FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_AT` / `STALE_EFFECTIVE_AT` / `SUBSCRIPTION_TRANSITION_NOT_ALLOWED`;
  - the transition insert trigger (`enforce_subscription_state_transition_sequence`) enforces the same policy for any insert path;
  - `get_organization_subscription_status(p_organization_id uuid) returns jsonb` — `authenticated` only (no `anon`/`public`/`service_role`), `SECURITY DEFINER`, empty `search_path`, `STABLE`, non-locking; `28000` for no subject/incoherent User; `ORGANIZATION_MANAGE_SUBSCRIPTION` decided before any Organization fact (unknown and inaccessible Organizations: identical `42501`); result exactly `{ organization_id, organization_display_name, subscription: null | { state, state_effective_at, valid_from, valid_until }, entitlements: {4 booleans} }`; the current (non-`EXPIRED`) relationship, else the most recently established period, `null` only if never established; stored state never replaced by an inferred one (a lapsed `ACTIVE` stays `ACTIVE`); no provider, source-event, idempotency, establishment-source, initiating-User or Subscription id;
  - `create_clinical_amendment` resolves the current Veterinarian context with `CLINICAL_DOCUMENTATION` capability but without an Entitlement requirement; Encounter creation keeps its `CLINICAL_CARE` Entitlement gate; signature, idempotency and ACL unchanged.
- Fixture/provisioning alignment (no policy change): `standard_appointment_duration_and_candidate_times_slice16.sql` now strips/restores the Entitlement with `ACTIVE → PAST_DUE → ACTIVE` recovery (its former `EXPIRED → ACTIVE` reactivation is no longer allowed); `production_validation_data_retire.sql` records `EXPIRED` at `statement_timestamp()` (`clock_timestamp()` is later than the statement instant and would be rejected as future-dated); `standard_appointment_duration_and_candidate_times_slice16.sql` and `scheduling_timezone_gating_and_structural_integrity_spec_apt_013.sql` now establish their Subscriptions at `statement_timestamp()` for the same reason; the validation dataset's finite `valid_until` (2026-12-31 23:00Z) is a documented, deliberate test-data kill-switch, not the customer model, and stays unchanged so re-runs remain idempotent.
- Mobile (JS only; no dependency, native or config change):
  - Professional → Management → `Suscripción / Subscription` entry, offered only with advisory Organization-scoped `ORGANIZATION_MANAGE_SUBSCRIPTION`; route `/professional/organizations/[organizationId]/subscription` (keyed per Organization, latest read wins, `42501` clears commercial content and returns calmly to Professional, recovery reflected on the next focus without sign-out);
  - calm state labels: Pendiente/Pending, Activa/Active, Problema de pago/Payment issue, Cancelada/Cancelled, Caducada/Expired; "since" instant; validity dates only when present; open-ended `ACTIVE` shows no end or renewal date; a lapsed `ACTIVE` stays "Activa" with "Su periodo de validez terminó…"; services shown separately from the state; strict parser rejects any extra member, so provider/internal data can never render;
  - Clinic settings "plan" copy replaced by neutral availability copy and, when the User may read it, a "Ver suscripción / View subscription" link; professional confirmation denial copy no longer mentions a plan; Guardian denial (request operation only): "Esta clínica no está aceptando solicitudes de cita en línea en este momento. / This clinic isn't currently accepting online appointment requests."; Guardian empty availability (request and reschedule), whatever the hidden reason: "No hay citas en línea disponibles para este periodo. / There are no online appointment times available for this period."
- Read-only operator diagnostic `supabase/provisioning/subscription_inventory_readonly.sql` (READ ONLY transaction, rolled back): Organizations with no Subscription ever, no current one, more than one current one, published Clinics without Appointment Entitlement, current status (open-ended / validity ended / ending within 30 days / `PAST_DUE` duration), historical future-dated or out-of-graph transitions, and historical establishments outside the Phase 1 initial-state rules. Locally positive-controlled with a committed-then-deleted fixture. It never reconciles anything.
- Operator runbook: `docs/SUBSCRIPTION_OPERATIONS_RUNBOOK.md`. Production security checkpoint for `20261020090000` prepared (not executed) in `docs/PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` §21.
- Trusted establishment (ADR-009 atomic, idempotent Organization + Owner + Subscription operation): **deliberately deferred to the next slice** — see the plan in "decisions and notes" below; the interim trusted path is unchanged.
- Validation: LOCAL in "Subscription Operational Safety — validation"; DEVELOPMENT in "Subscription Operational Safety — DEVELOPMENT closure" below.

#### Subscription Operational Safety — validation

LOCAL, recorded before the DEVELOPMENT rollout (clean `supabase db reset --local`); these remain the authoritative automated figures:

- all **37 SQL suites PASS** with zero public/Auth data rows, including the new `subscription_operational_safety_spec_sub_002_003.sql` (25-pair graph with `PENDING → EXPIRED`, establishment initial-state and future-time rules, replay and contradictory reuse, terminal, future/stale/equal effective times, trigger backstop, idempotency, non-inheritance, open-ended `ACTIVE`, exact Entitlement boundaries, new period after `EXPIRED`, status read, public/Guardian boundary) and `clinical_amendment_entitlement_continuity_spec_sub_004.sql`; the adjusted Slice 16 suite passes;
- **27/27 Subscription Operational Safety mutants caught** (`run_subscription_safety_faults.py`: 23 Subscription — including `PENDING → EXPIRED` removed, `PENDING → CANCELLED` added, establishment initial state and future time, and a candidate read that distinguishes Entitlement loss — + 4 Amendment continuity);
- **7 real two-session transition races PASS** (`run_subscription_transition_concurrency.py`): PAST_DUE/CANCELLED in both orders, a transition racing an expiry, a duplicate source event (one transition, same result), recovery vs expiry in both orders, a `PENDING` closure racing an activation; one coherent history each, at most one current relationship, no deadlock;
- regression unchanged and PASS: temporal faults 14/14 + 5/5, Staff 19/19 (+ simulated interruption), B1 31/31 + races, B2 29/29 + races, Slice C 19/19 + races, Add Clinic 20/20 + races, Edge faults 15/15, harness unit tests 9/9; Communications Deno 23/23 (Edge code untouched);
- read-only inventory script positive-controlled locally (committed-then-deleted fixture; zero retained rows);
- mobile: Jest **95 suites / 2,011 tests PASS** (adds the reason-agnostic empty-availability copy and bare empty candidate result pins); **10/10 ad hoc mobile mutants caught** (earlier pass); typecheck, strict unused-locals/parameters, ESLint, Prettier PASS; `git diff --check` clean.

#### Subscription Operational Safety — DEVELOPMENT closure (user-confirmed)

- **Status: CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT.** This closes operational safety of the trusted, manually managed Phase 1 Subscription model only. It does not complete commercial onboarding and adds no billing automation. **Production has not been deployed or touched.**
- Rollout performed by the user: applied `20261031090000_subscription_operational_safety.sql` to DEVELOPMENT and loaded the mobile JS through Metro on the existing Android development build. No Edge Function redeploy, secret, native dependency or new Android build was required.
- Read-only inventory (`subscription_inventory_readonly.sql`), run before the migration: the relevant Organization was reported with current state `ACTIVE`, open-ended validity (`valid_until = NULL`), validity note `OPEN_ENDED`. Only this Organization's result is recorded here; no legacy reconciliation was performed.
- DEVELOPMENT function attributes and ACLs (checked directly):
  - `apply_subscription_state_transition(...)`: `SECURITY DEFINER` true; `VOLATILE`; `search_path=""`; `anon` EXECUTE false; `authenticated` EXECUTE false; `service_role` EXECUTE true;
  - `establish_organization_subscription(...)`: `SECURITY DEFINER` true; `VOLATILE`; `search_path=""`; `anon` EXECUTE false; `authenticated` EXECUTE false; `service_role` EXECUTE true;
  - `get_organization_subscription_status(uuid)`: `SECURITY DEFINER` true; `STABLE`; `search_path=""`; `anon` EXECUTE false; `authenticated` EXECUTE true; `service_role` EXECUTE false;
  - `subscription_transition_is_allowed(text,text)`: not `SECURITY DEFINER`; `IMMUTABLE`; `search_path=""`; `anon`/`authenticated`/`service_role` EXECUTE false;
  - `create_clinical_amendment(...)`: `SECURITY DEFINER` true; `VOLATILE`; `search_path=""`; `authenticated` EXECUTE true; `service_role` EXECUTE true; `anon` EXECUTE false.
  No other ACL is recorded as checked.
- Authentication boundary: a direct SQL Editor call to `get_organization_subscription_status(...)` returned `28000 Authentication is required`, because the SQL Editor does not run with an authenticated application subject — confirmation that the read does not bypass authentication, not an implementation fault.
- Physical Android validation (user-confirmed), on the existing Subscription of the tested Organization, without logout/login or token refresh at any point. The account used holds both Professional and Guardian contexts, which let both sides be checked without switching accounts (a convenience of this test, not a product requirement):
  - **`ACTIVE → PAST_DUE`**, applied as a trusted DEVELOPMENT operation with `apply_subscription_state_transition`: the Professional Subscription screen showed the `PAST_DUE` state ("Problema de pago"); the affected professional Entitlements became unavailable immediately, with no stale JWT authority preserving those commercial capabilities; the Clinic remained published and publicly discoverable; Guardian/public surfaces exposed no Subscription, payment, plan or Entitlement state;
  - **Guardian privacy** during `PAST_DUE`: the published Clinic stayed visible in discovery; candidate-time unavailability showed the generic, reason-agnostic message ("No hay citas en línea disponibles para este periodo." / "There are no online appointment times available for this period."); no commercial reason, Subscription state, `PAST_DUE`, Entitlement or payment information was disclosed. The approved distinction stands: an empty requestable-times result only ever shows that generic message, while a request-level denial may show the non-commercial "Esta clínica no está aceptando solicitudes de cita en línea en este momento."; no backend or public commercial signal exists;
  - **`PAST_DUE → ACTIVE`** recovery of the same Subscription, with a new current `valid_from` and `valid_until = NULL`: the Subscription screen returned to "Activa" with no artificial expiry or renewal date; the professional Entitlements returned and Appointment functionality became available again; Clinic publication and discovery stayed stable throughout.
- Final DEVELOPMENT state of the tested Subscription: `ACTIVE`, open-ended.
- Device vs automated validation: the device pass covered the real `ACTIVE → PAST_DUE → ACTIVE` lifecycle and the Professional/Guardian behaviour above. The automated LOCAL suites remain authoritative for everything else, at least: `PENDING → EXPIRED`; forbidden transition pairs; `EXPIRED` terminal behaviour; invalid initial Subscription states; future-dated initial-state rejection; future-dated transition rejection; exact temporal boundaries; transition races; Amendment continuity after Entitlement loss; provider/internal-data leakage; legacy inventory diagnostics. No full local rerun was performed after the DEVELOPMENT device validation; the LOCAL figures above, recorded before rollout, remain the authoritative automated evidence.
- Final approved Phase 1 Subscription policy:
  - a normal manually managed `ACTIVE` Subscription is open-ended; there is no periodic renewal operation in this manual phase; finite validity remains representable when a real commercial boundary is known;
  - `PENDING` may become `ACTIVE` or `EXPIRED`; `ACTIVE` may become `PAST_DUE`, `CANCELLED` or `EXPIRED`; `PAST_DUE` may recover to `ACTIVE` or close to `EXPIRED`; `CANCELLED` may only become `EXPIRED`; `EXPIRED` is terminal;
  - future-dated state transitions are forbidden; an initial Subscription state is `PENDING` or `ACTIVE` only, and future initial effective times are forbidden;
  - `PAST_DUE` grants no Entitlement (no grace) and never expires automatically;
  - published Clinics are not unpublished automatically on commercial lapse; Guardian candidate-time emptiness remains reason-agnostic;
  - necessary clinical Amendments remain possible after commercial Entitlement loss under the existing professional authorization rules.
- Remaining Subscription/commercial work (closing this slice does not close commercial onboarding): ADR-009 atomic, idempotent Organization + Owner + initial Subscription establishment; a trusted Owner-management path; legacy Organization inventory/reconciliation if any are found; Production migration rollout; billing-provider integration; billing self-service; plan/tier modelling. Periodic renewal is not missing Phase 1 functionality: the approved manual `ACTIVE` model is open-ended.
- **Next prioritized area: trusted establishment hardening** — not commercial onboarding UI. Goal: replace today's manually composed Organization + initial Owner Membership + OWNER Responsibility + initial Subscription with the atomic, idempotent trusted establishment boundary ADR-009 already requires (plan under "decisions and notes" below). No billing provider, checkout, pricing, plans or self-service Organization signup is chosen. After it, the intended direction remains: Staff Responsibility editing + Veterinarian enablement; a minimal clinical-record flow from the Appointment detail; then the remaining Phase 1 clinical completeness work.

#### Subscription Operational Safety — decisions and notes

- **D8 resolved (privacy-preserving).** The former Guardian-message open decision is closed by the finalized policy: the uniform empty requestable-times result stays indistinguishable (`SPEC-APT-014` BR-5, `SPEC-SUB-003` §15) and is shown with one generic, reason-agnostic message; only the request operation's own unavailability outcome produces "not accepting online appointment requests". No new public/Guardian signal of Subscription or Entitlement state exists.
- **Trusted establishment deferred (plan).** ADR-009's atomic, idempotent operation needs choices left open by ADR-009 itself: how the designated Owner is identified without session impersonation (domain User id vs Auth subject vs email) and the idempotency anchor (reuse `organization_subscriptions.establishment_idempotency_key` to recover the Organization on retry vs a new Organization-level key). Plan: one `service_role` operation `(owner, display name, initial state, effective time, valid_from, valid_until, establishment source, idempotency key, provider references)` returning `{ organization_id, membership_id, subscription_id, outcome: ESTABLISHED | ALREADY_ESTABLISHED }`, Organization-serialized, contradictory reuse `23505`; its own rollback-only suite, faults, and races; then the client-free `create_veterinary_organization` becomes compatibility-only.
- **Concurrency semantics (non-blocking, ADR-006 as specified):** the trusted operation derives `from_state` itself and takes no expected-state guard, so an operator's `EXPIRED` racing a committed recovery is applied as `ACTIVE → EXPIRED` (allowed). Callers who need intent protection should re-read state first (runbook §2).
- **PENDING closure:** `PENDING → EXPIRED` is the terminal closure of a relationship that never became active; there is no `PENDING → CANCELLED`.
- **Establishment backstop (non-blocking):** the initial-state rules live in the trusted establishment operation, the only insert path; there is deliberately no insert trigger on `organization_subscriptions` (direct trusted inserts and rollback-only data fixtures are not policed). The inventory reports historical establishments outside the rules.
- **Edge (unchanged, pre-existing):** with the locally installed Deno 2.9.6, `clinical-attachments` tests fail type-checking (`TS2345 Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>` vs `BufferSource`) without any source change in this slice; Communications Deno tests pass 23/23.

### Trusted Organization Establishment Hardening (ADR-009 amended, SPEC-ORG-001 BR-16/§10, SPEC-SUB-001 §14)

**CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT.** Implemented and fully validated LOCALLY; migration `20261101090000_trusted_organization_establishment.sql` applied to DEVELOPMENT by the user, and the key establishment behaviour validated there (see "DEVELOPMENT closure" below). **Production has not been deployed or touched.** No commercial onboarding UI, self-service Organization signup, billing provider, checkout, pricing or plan exists.

- Why: Organization + initial Owner Membership + `OWNER` responsibility + initial Subscription were composed manually by an operator session that impersonated the Owner and called two independent operations, with no idempotency for the Organization (ADR-009 required one atomic, idempotent trusted operation).
- Approved decisions (EST-1…EST-11), recorded authoritatively in ADR-009 (amendment), SPEC-ORG-001 BR-16 and §10, and SPEC-SUB-001 §14:
  - the Owner is identified by the domain User id (`p_owner_user_id`), never an Auth subject, JWT subject or email; email resolution happens only in a read-only operator preflight;
  - initial-Owner account eligibility (scoped to Commercial Onboarding only): domain User exists, one coherent mapped Auth account, confirmed email, not deleted, not anonymous, not currently banned (`banned_until` null or `<= t`); no Profile required;
  - one caller-generated UUID is the idempotency anchor, stored on the Organization and reused as the initial Subscription's establishment key; display name, email, provider references, timestamps and generated ids are never anchors;
  - exact replay compares the requested inputs (omitted vs explicit initial effective time are different requests; provider references compared as an order-insensitive set); any material difference is `23505`;
  - one establishment instant `t = statement_timestamp()`;
  - no structural Organization → Subscription invariant yet; legacy Organizations not reconciled.
- Migration `20261101090000_trusted_organization_establishment.sql` (no backfill, no data change):
  - `veterinary_organizations.establishment_idempotency_key uuid` (nullable, unique) and `establishment_request_digest bytea` (SHA-256 of the canonical requested inputs; both null or both set; immutable via the Organization immutability trigger);
  - `establish_veterinary_organization(p_owner_user_id uuid, p_organization_display_name text, p_establishment_idempotency_key uuid, p_initial_subscription_state text, p_initial_state_effective_at timestamptz, p_commercial_valid_from timestamptz, p_commercial_valid_until timestamptz, p_establishment_source text, p_provider_references jsonb default null) returns jsonb` — `SECURITY DEFINER`, empty `search_path`, `VOLATILE`, EXECUTE `service_role` only (revoked from `public`, `anon`, `authenticated`). Order: shape (`22023`) → exact replay / contradictory reuse (`23505` `ESTABLISHMENT_KEY_CONFLICT`, also for a key already used by another Subscription) → Owner identity (`28000` `OWNER_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE`) and eligibility (`42501` `OWNER_ACCOUNT_INELIGIBLE`) → initial policy (`23514` `INITIAL_STATE_NOT_ALLOWED` / `FUTURE_EFFECTIVE_AT`) → writes. Creates exactly the Organization (with key and digest), the Owner Membership (start `t`, no end, no Invitation), the active Organization-scoped `OWNER` Grant (start `t`, granted by the Owner as before), the Subscription (`established_at = t`, state effective at the request or `t`, requested validity, no initiating User) and the requested provider references (`recorded_at = t`). No Clinic or anything else. Result `{organization_id, owner_membership_id, subscription_id, outcome: ESTABLISHED | ALREADY_ESTABLISHED}`; nothing else, and no message carries the key, an email, an Auth subject or a provider reference;
  - concurrency: identical calls serialize on the unique Organization key (`INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, then re-read of the committed Organization and digest comparison); no advisory lock;
  - constraint surfacing: before reporting success, the operation runs `SET CONSTRAINTS … IMMEDIATE` on exactly the ten named deferred constraint triggers its writes queue (active Owner, Grant minimum, Grant within Membership, Invitation origin, single current Subscription), then restores them to their declared `DEFERRED` mode. `ESTABLISHED` is therefore never reported for an establishment that would fail at commit. Side effect, accepted and documented: pending events of those same constraints queued earlier by the caller's own transaction are also checked at that point (they would fail at commit anyway). The operational contract is still one autocommit call;
  - private helpers (no EXECUTE for `public`/`anon`/`authenticated`/`service_role`): `assert_initial_owner_eligible`, `replay_veterinary_organization_establishment`, `canonical_subscription_provider_references`, `stored_subscription_provider_references`;
  - `establish_organization_subscription` keeps its signature, result and `service_role` ACL for a **later** Subscription period after `EXPIRED`; its exact replay now also compares the provider-reference set (identical or reordered references replay; added, removed, changed or omitted references are `23505`), and its conflict message no longer echoes the key;
  - `create_veterinary_organization(text)` is **dropped**: every caller migrated, and it has no reusable helper value (it derived the Owner from the caller's subject and established no Subscription).
- Callers migrated: `professional_authority_create_then_act.sql`, `scheduling_timezone_gating_and_structural_integrity_spec_apt_013.sql`, `standard_appointment_duration_and_candidate_times_slice16.sql` (Organization B now `PENDING`, which preserves its "no Entitlement" intent), `run_temporal_authority_faults.py` (the establishment-instant mutant now targets the new operation); the obsolete `organization_establishment_trusted_only_adr_009.sql` (it exercised only the dropped function) is replaced by `trusted_organization_establishment_adr_009.sql`.
- Provisioning: `production_validation_data.sql` establishes the dataset with ONE `establish_veterinary_organization` call for the Owner's domain User (resolved read-only from `owner_email`), identifies the dataset by its establishment key (never by display name), replays it on re-run (`ALREADY_ESTABLISHED`, same ids), and recognizes a dataset provisioned before this migration by its Subscription key (verified and reconfigured, never re-established). Owner impersonation remains only for the Owner's own later configuration operations (Professional Profile, Clinics, schedules), never for establishment. Preflight now requires `20261101090000`. The retire script is unchanged and compatible (it identifies the dataset by Subscription key and source).
- Read-only inventory: new section 9 `ESTABLISHMENT_PATH` (`ATOMIC_ESTABLISHMENT` / `PRE_ATOMIC_PATH`, with whether the Organization ever had a Subscription), read through `to_jsonb` so it also runs on databases without the new column (Production). Nothing is reconciled.
- Docs: ADR-009, SPEC-ORG-001, SPEC-SUB-001, `SUBSCRIPTION_OPERATIONS_RUNBOOK.md` §3.1 (one autocommit call, no impersonation; §3.7 later periods only), `PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` §20/§21, `ARCHITECTURE.md` §19, `RELATIONAL_MODEL.md`, `TESTING.md`.

#### Trusted Organization Establishment — delivered boundary and notes

- **Delivered trusted boundary.** One `service_role`-only, atomic and idempotent operation, `establish_veterinary_organization`, creates exactly: (1) the Veterinary Organization; (2) the initial Owner Membership; (3) the active Organization-scoped `OWNER` Responsibility; (4) the initial Subscription; (5) optional provider references; (6) the durable establishment idempotency metadata on the Organization. It does **not** create a Clinic, Clinic profile or location, Staff Invitation, Veterinarian Professional Profile, `VETERINARIAN` Responsibility, plan or billing checkout.
- **Owner identity.** The initial Owner is identified by domain User id. The account must map coherently between domain User and Auth, have a confirmed email, and not be deleted, anonymous or currently banned; no Profile is required. This rule is scoped to initial Organization ownership and does not apply to other identity workflows.
- **Idempotency.** The caller supplies one UUID per establishment intent; it is stored on the Organization and reused as the initial Subscription's establishment key. The replay fingerprint is computed from the requested semantic inputs (never from generated ids or current timestamps), including provider references. Exact replay returns the original ids with `ALREADY_ESTABLISHED`; contradictory reuse is a deterministic conflict.
- **Atomicity and concurrency** (LOCAL evidence above): Organization, Membership, `OWNER` Grant and Subscription are one atomic footprint; injected failures at each write stage leave no partial data; identical concurrent calls create one footprint; conflicting same-key calls yield one establishment and one deterministic conflict; different keys for the same Owner both succeed; no deadlocks. The documented operator invocation is one autocommit call (`SUBSCRIPTION_OPERATIONS_RUNBOOK.md` §3.1).
- **Old and retained operations.** `create_veterinary_organization(text)` is removed and is no longer an Organization-establishment path. `establish_organization_subscription(...)` remains `service_role`-only for a later **new** Subscription period after `EXPIRED`. No current runbook tells operators to establish an Organization through Owner impersonation, `SET ROLE`, separate Organization and initial-Subscription calls, or display-name rerun detection.
- **Legacy / invariant boundary.** No global Organization → Subscription structural constraint was added. Organizations established through the trusted operation satisfy the invariant prospectively; legacy Organizations are untouched; reconciliation stays deferred until real inventory results justify a product decision; no historical or backdated Subscription is ever fabricated.
- **Operational/commercial milestone.** MenizVet now has a trusted, atomic and retry-safe way to establish the initial commercial Organization boundary. With the already-closed operational self-service, an operator establishes Organization + Owner + initial Subscription in one call, and the Owner then continues normal Organization setup in the app. This does **not** make commercial onboarding self-service. Still deferred: checkout, billing-provider integration, pricing, plans/tiers, public Organization signup, and legacy Organization reconciliation.
- **Production.** Not deployed or touched. `20261101090000` remains pending there, behind the existing ordered Production rollout and its read-only inventory prerequisite (`PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md` §21).

Typed times are civil date/time in the Appointment's scheduling timezone (DST gaps rejected, overlaps reported); no native picker or dependency; no new build. ES/EN copy.

Backend, SQL and Specs did not change in those passes, so the SQL figures above are not superseded and no SQL rerun was required for them.
- Existing Phase-1 behaviour recorded, not introduced: an Appointment-derived Care Authorization has no normal-end operation, so post-care documentation stays possible while it is ACTIVE (SPEC-ACC-002 BR-55).
- **Delivered clinical loop and milestone.** Appointment → start/open clinical care → Encounter → one or more Clinical Entries → Draft → Finalize → Guardian medical history. For the first time in DEVELOPMENT, an attended Appointment can produce a real finalized clinical record through the app, and that finalized Entry appears in the Guardian's Pet medical history without SQL. This is not full EMR completion and not Clinical Phase 1 completion.
- Lifecycle independence (pinned): Appointment COMPLETED is independent from clinical documentation. Starting an Encounter does not Complete the Appointment; finalizing an Entry does not Complete it; concluding an Encounter does not Complete it; completing an Appointment does not automatically Conclude an Encounter; concluding an Encounter does not automatically Finalize Drafts.
- Guardian publication: Draft Entries are not visible to Guardians. A Clinical Entry becomes visible to the Guardian immediately when it is FINALIZED — Appointment COMPLETED is not required and Encounter CONCLUDED is not required. The existing Guardian medical-history code needed no product change for this slice. Finalize publishes that one Entry; it does not close the whole clinical record.
- Encounter start (validated client contract):
  - CONFIRMED Appointment: the UI offers `Empezar ahora` and `Indicar otra hora`; `Empezar ahora` sends NULL and the server resolves its statement instant;
  - COMPLETED Appointment: retrospective documentation is supported and the Veterinarian must explicitly establish the real care-start instant (NULL is never sent). After the DEVELOPMENT UX polish, if the scheduled Appointment start lies inside the server-provided valid care-start window, `A la hora prevista · HH:MM` is an explicit one-tap confirmation that sends the exact scheduled instant; otherwise, or by choice, `A otra hora` reveals the manual controls. A same-day window fixes the date as context and asks only for the time; a multi-day window preselects the scheduled Clinic-local date only when it lies inside the valid date range, otherwise no date is chosen automatically; the time always starts empty. The server remains authoritative for temporal validity.
- Schedule-read integration: `get_clinic_appointment_schedule` exposes `has_clinical_encounter` — operational existence only, no clinical content, no audit. For a CONFIRMED Appointment that has an Encounter, Cancel, No-show and Propose a new time are hidden where the Appointment rules make them invalid, and Complete remains independently governed by the Appointment rules. No clinical content is exposed to Receptionist/Admin schedule users.
- Draft idempotency (delivered safety contract): Draft creation uses a caller-generated UUID; the creation request has a stable immutable digest; an exact replay returns the same Entry; the same key with different requested creation facts is rejected deterministically (`KEY_CONFLICT`); later Draft edits do not invalidate the replay comparison; opening the editor creates nothing and the first explicit Save Draft creates the row. This closes the lost-response duplicate-Draft risk.
- One-Encounter invariant: one Encounter per Care Authorization is now structurally enforced. The unique constraint is a backstop for what was already the SPEC-MED-001 invariant; local data had no duplicates. Production requires the documented preflight before eventual rollout (see `PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT.md`); Production has not been checked.
- Entry model: several Clinical Entries per Encounter; categories EXAMINATION, DIAGNOSIS, TREATMENT, PROCEDURE, VACCINATION, PRESCRIPTION, LABORATORY_RESULT, IMAGING_RESULT, CLINICAL_NOTE and OTHER; no category is preselected; OTHER requires context; `clinical_event_at` remains optional. No structured diagnosis, medication or vitals model was added.
- Collaboration: the responsible Veterinarian may create new Draft Entries. Another currently authorized Veterinarian at the Clinic may update an existing Draft, finalize and conclude, but may not create a new Draft for that Encounter. Neither Appointment Veterinarian Assignment nor Veterinarian Availability is required.
- Conclude (`Terminar atención` / `Mark care as finished`) marks the active care episode as concluded. It does not Complete the Appointment, Finalize Drafts, seal the medical record or end the Care Authorization; post-conclusion documentation remains possible where the existing domain allows it.
- Subscription continuity (already-approved model, covered by automated tests, not reopened): if clinical Entitlement is lost, starting a new Encounter is denied calmly; for an existing Encounter, the clinical read, Draft create/update, Finalize and Conclude continue while current professional authority permits; Guardian history remains available.
- Android validation findings corrected after physical testing (UX only — no domain, lifecycle, SQL, Spec, temporal or authorization rule changed):
  - clinical copy polish: a missing category now reads `Selecciona una categoría para poder finalizar esta entrada.` (a Draft can still be saved without a category; the category/content rules for Finalize did not change); a concluded Encounter where documentation is still allowed reads `La atención ha terminado. Puedes seguir completando el registro clínico si queda información pendiente.`, and where the server flags allow no further change a calm read-only message says no information can currently be added or edited; implementation-style wording such as `cuando las reglas lo permiten` was removed, including from the `Terminar atención` confirmation; the no-valid-care-start message became neutral and user-facing (`No es posible iniciar un registro clínico para esta visita.`, with a non-technical explanation that the care date and current Clinic access do not permit creating the record);
  - completed-visit care-start polish: before, the COMPLETED flow showed a raw Date field, a raw Time field, the full allowed interval, the visible IANA timezone and a technical CTA; after, it asks `¿Cuándo empezó realmente la atención?` with the one-tap scheduled-time shortcut when server-valid, manual controls only after `A otra hora`, time-only entry for a same-day window, an explicit date choice for a multi-day window when the scheduled date is not a defensible valid default, and the scheduling timezone stays authoritative but is no longer primary UI. The explicit care-start confirmation remains and the shortcut is hidden whenever the scheduled start is outside the server window (for example, a fixture scheduled at 12:00 whose valid window opens at 12:32 offers only `A otra hora`);
  - Appointment-detail note: the `Atención clínica iniciada` note has been removed from the Appointment detail in every lifecycle. `has_clinical_encounter` is deliberately true for an ACTIVE and a CONCLUDED Encounter alike (SPEC-APT-012 BR-48), so the note went stale after Terminar atención on a still-CONFIRMED Appointment; no Encounter-state field was added to the schedule and the detail performs no clinical-record read. Nothing replaces it (no `Atención terminada`, no inferred Encounter state). The Encounter's actual state is shown inside the Clinical Record screen (SPEC-MED-009), and the Clinical Record button, the action suppression, and Complete are unchanged.
- Historical fixture finding (not a product defect): an older COMPLETED fixture in DEVELOPMENT initially showed no usable care-start instant. The server correctly reported that no valid care-start instant existed for that Veterinarian context, the rule was not weakened, and the copy-polish pass did not alter the fixture. The mobile receives only the aggregate no-valid-care-start result, so it cannot attribute the cause to one specific temporal factor and the final copy says nothing more specific; this document does not attribute it to the Clinic Assignment either. Retrospective documentation is validated on a COMPLETED Appointment that has a valid care-start window.
- DEVELOPMENT state and ACL verification (checked by the user in DEVELOPMENT; nothing below is claimed beyond what was physically checked):
  - applied: `20261103090000_minimal_clinical_record.sql`;
  - client-safe authenticated writes — `start_appointment_clinical_encounter(uuid,uuid,timestamptz)`, `create_appointment_clinical_draft(uuid,uuid,uuid,text,text,text,timestamptz)`, `update_appointment_clinical_draft(uuid,uuid,uuid,bigint,text,text,text,timestamptz)`, `finalize_appointment_clinical_entry(uuid,uuid,uuid,bigint)` and `conclude_appointment_clinical_encounter(uuid,uuid)`: each SECURITY DEFINER, VOLATILE, `search_path=""`, `anon` EXECUTE false, `authenticated` EXECUTE true, `service_role` EXECUTE false;
  - client-safe read — `view_appointment_clinical_record(uuid,uuid)`: SECURITY DEFINER, VOLATILE, `search_path=""`, `anon` false, `authenticated` true, `service_role` false. VOLATILE is intentional: the read emits a medical-access audit event when clinical content is returned;
  - internal helper — `appointment_clinical_care_window(uuid)`: SECURITY DEFINER, STABLE, `search_path=""`, `anon`, `authenticated` and `service_role` all false;
  - legacy/internal generic operations — `create_clinical_encounter(...)`, `create_draft_clinical_record_entry(...)`, `update_draft_clinical_record_entry(...)`, `finalize_clinical_record_entry(...)`, `conclude_clinical_encounter(...)` and `view_care_authorization_clinical_work_context(...)`: `authenticated` EXECUTE false, `service_role` EXECUTE true, `anon` false — no longer client paths; service-role/internal only.

The backend permits a Clinic proposal earlier than the currently requested date provided it is still in the future and feasible, and the mobile already starts the candidate search from Clinic-local today; no product-code defect was confirmed, and the observation was affected by the DEVELOPMENT Clinic's timezone configuration during testing. The observation occurred while the DEVELOPMENT Clinic had a non-representative test timezone configured. No scheduling code defect was confirmed; backend and mobile regression tests proved that REQUESTED alternative-time candidates may include valid Clinic-local times earlier than the requested date. Regression coverage was added (a real-clock section in the alternative-time SQL suite and a route-level Jest file for both candidate screens).

### Clinical Amendments — Phase 1 (SPEC-MED-004 §15/§16, SPEC-MED-009 BR-4/BR-5)

- Status: **Clinical Amendments — Phase 1: CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT.**
  - Implemented and fully validated locally; the user applied `20261104090000_clinical_amendments.sql` to DEVELOPMENT, checked the ACLs there, and physically validated the key Amendment workflow on Android (details below).
  - **Production has not been deployed or touched.** `20261104090000_clinical_amendments.sql` remains pending there.
  - **Clinical Phase 1 remains OPEN.** Next: **Professional longitudinal / cross-Clinic history + Veterinarian provenance identity.** Attachments remain P1/deferred and are not required for this closure.
- Final Amendment semantics (product concept: a *correction / amendment*, never a direct edit): the original FINALIZED Entry remains immutable and is never overwritten; an Amendment is append-only; several Amendments per Entry are allowed; each preserves its own provenance (amending User, amending Clinic Assignment, creation time) beside the original authorship; existing Amendments cannot be edited or deleted.
- Idempotency: the first request → `CREATED`; an exact replay or lost-response retry → `ALREADY_CREATED` with the same `amendment_id`; the same key with a changed request → deterministic `23505 KEY_CONFLICT` (key never echoed); no duplicate correction for the same intent.
- Authorization / confidentiality: authority is established before target existence or lifecycle is revealed; foreign, unknown and inaccessible targets fail with one uniform `42501 Clinical record unavailable`; the old generic writer `create_clinical_amendment` is no longer executable by authenticated clients; the client uses the Appointment-keyed wrapper; Care Authorization, User and Clinic Assignment ids are never client inputs.
- Lifecycle independence: an Amendment does not reopen the Encounter, does not change the Appointment state, does not mutate the original Entry, and does not require the original author to remain active; another current, same-Clinic authorized Veterinarian may amend.
- D2 access basis (final): the authenticated client Amendment wrapper and the SPEC-MED-009 read use the same server-side predicate (`appointment_clinical_record_accessible`). Allowed: an ACTIVE Encounter under the continuity rules; a CONCLUDED Encounter while its Care Authorization remains ACTIVE. Denied: CONCLUDED Encounter + REVOKED Care Authorization; CONCLUDED Encounter + ENDED Care Authorization. A cached or known `entry_id` does not bypass the revoked access boundary, and no Amendment-only read was introduced, so a Guardian revocation closes client access cleanly. Subscription lapse is a separate concern and does not itself revoke Amendment continuity.
- Subscription continuity (approved policy, not reopened): Amendment creation does not require a current Subscription Entitlement once the record is otherwise accessible under the approved clinical continuity contract.
- Approved authority rule (product decision, implemented as given): a finalized Entry stays immutable; corrections are immutable Amendments; a Veterinarian with current `CLINICAL_DOCUMENTATION` authority in the Entry's Clinic may amend; not required — original author still active or being the author, responsible-Veterinarian status, Appointment Veterinarian Assignment, Availability, ACTIVE Encounter, open/CONFIRMED Appointment, or current Entitlement (Subscription continuity of SPEC-SUB-004 BR-20 unchanged).
- Approved D2 (read/write basis; supersedes the earlier "Care Authorization never affects client Amendment availability"): a client Amendment is possible only while the Entry is reachable through the SPEC-MED-009 access basis — Encounter ACTIVE (revocation-safe continuity) or Care Authorization ACTIVE. COMPLETED Appointment + CONCLUDED Encounter + ACTIVE Care Authorization: allowed. CONCLUDED Encounter + REVOKED/ENDED Care Authorization: not available from the app, enforced in the wrapper, not only in mobile. No Amendment-only read exists.
- Pre-implementation audit: the existing Amendment model (table, append-only trigger, per-User idempotency key, provenance) and `create_clinical_amendment`'s authority (same-Clinic current Veterinarian context with `CLINICAL_DOCUMENTATION`, no Entitlement) already matched the rule; the client-contract gaps were existence-before-authority (`23503` for an unknown Entry), a bare UUID result, and no Appointment-keyed path. Guardian history already returned Amendments. No product contradiction found.
- Amendment semantics (unchanged): additive record — reason + amendment content (trimmed, non-blank) + amending User + amending Clinic Assignment + creation time — referencing the original FINALIZED Entry; several per Entry, ordered by creation time then id; no category, event time or structured fields; no update/delete (trigger), the Entry stays frozen (Entry trigger). Semantic identity of a key: Entry, reason, content, amending Clinic Assignment.
- Specs: SPEC-MED-004 §15 (client contract BR-39–BR-44 + mobile presentation) and §16 (Notes 22–31); SPEC-MED-009 BR-4/BR-5 (Amendments, `may_amend`, one audit event) and Notes 11–12; SPEC-MED-003 §15 mobile note (the read-only copy may now point to an Amendment). RELATIONAL_MODEL "Create Amendment" row gains the client path (no schema change).
- Migration `20261104090000_clinical_amendments.sql` (no table/constraint/trigger change):
  - `create_appointment_clinical_amendment(p_clinic_id, p_appointment_id, p_entry_id, p_idempotency_key, p_reason, p_amendment_content) → {outcome, amendment_id}`: SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=""`, authenticated only (`public`, `anon`, `service_role` revoked). Order: authenticate → lock current exact-Clinic Veterinarian context (`lock_clinical_actor_context`, `CLINICAL_DOCUMENTATION`) → resolve the Entry within that Clinic's Appointment Encounter → lock Care Authorization, Encounter, Entry (`for share`) and require the shared SPEC-MED-009 predicate `appointment_clinical_record_accessible(encounter)` (Encounter ACTIVE or Care Authorization ACTIVE; private, no client EXECUTE; also used by the read) → FINALIZED (`23514 ENTRY_NOT_FINALIZED`) → `22023 KEY_REQUIRED / REASON_REQUIRED / CONTENT_REQUIRED` → insert `on conflict do nothing` → `CREATED`, or `ALREADY_CREATED` for an identical repeat, or `23505 KEY_CONFLICT` (key never echoed). Missing authority, every unknown/foreign Clinic, Organization, Appointment, Encounter or Entry, and a record no longer accessible (including a stale Entry id or an exact replay after revocation): one uniform `42501 Clinical record unavailable`. Lock order Organization → Clinic → Membership/Grant/Assignment → Care Authorization → Encounter → Entry;
  - `view_appointment_clinical_record` (same signature/ACL): each Entry gains `amendments` (≤100, oldest first: `amendment_id`, `reason`, `amendment_content`, `created_at`, `amended_by_me`) and `amendments_truncated`; the Encounter gains `may_amend`; still exactly one audit event; no User/Assignment id;
  - authenticated EXECUTE withdrawn from `create_clinical_amendment` (service_role kept); no mobile client ever called it.
- Access matrix (D2, enforced server-side by the shared predicate): Encounter ACTIVE + Care Authorization ACTIVE/REVOKED → read permitted, Amendment allowed; Encounter CONCLUDED + Care Authorization ACTIVE (including COMPLETED Appointment) → read permitted, Amendment allowed; Encounter CONCLUDED + Care Authorization REVOKED or ENDED → read DENIED, Amendment `42501`, nothing inserted. Revocation changes no Entry, Amendment or provenance; a denied Amendment attempt returns no content and writes no audit event of its own. The former OPEN DECISION is resolved by D2.
- Guardian: the original finalized Entry is visible, Amendments are visible, Drafts are invisible; no Guardian product-code change was required for this slice. Known non-blocking privacy follow-up (**not fixed**): the existing Guardian history payload still contains internal User / Clinic Assignment ids (`amending_user_id`, `amending_veterinarian_clinic_assignment_id`, Entry author/finalizer User and Assignment ids), even though the app does not display them.
- Mobile: a FINALIZED Entry shows its Amendments beneath it (reason, content, added time, Tú/Otro veterinario) and, when `may_amend`, **Añadir corrección / Add amendment** (never on a Draft); the read-only note now says "Si necesitas corregirla, añade una corrección."; Entry cards show `Correcciones: N`. New route `[appointmentId]/clinical-amendment`: the original Entry stays visible; only reason and corrected/added information; a review stating permanence, preservation of the original and Guardian visibility; one key per form session (a lost response is resubmitted with the same key, or reconciled from the re-read); calm `KEY_CONFLICT`; authoritative re-read after every outcome; a `DENIED` re-read (e.g. after revocation) drops the form and all clinical content for the existing calm unavailable state, with no hint that the Entry exists. The Entry review block was extracted into `clinical-entry-review.tsx` for reuse. No dependency, no native change, no new build.
- Callers migrated: the MED-001 and SUB-004 suites call `create_clinical_amendment` as the trusted owner; the MCR suite pins the withdrawn grant and the two Amendment keys per Entry; `run_clinical_record_faults.py` takes the read base from `20261104090000`.
- Automated validation (LOCAL, clean `supabase db reset --local`): **43/43 SQL suites PASS** (new `clinical_amendments_spec_med_004.sql`), zero public/Auth rows afterwards; **29/29 Amendment deliberate faults caught** (`run_clinical_amendment_faults.py`, including 6 D2 access-basis mutants); **9/9 real two-session Amendment races PASS** (`run_clinical_amendment_concurrency.py`, including Guardian revoke vs post-care Amendment in both orders), no deadlock, zero retained fixtures; regressions PASS: clinical record faults 42/42 and races 18/18, Subscription 27/27, Workforce 45/45, Trusted Establishment 25/25, Edge invitation 15/15, temporal, Staff read/B1/B2/End access, Add Clinic and every other race runner (all runners exit 0; after D2 the full SQL suite, clinical, Subscription and harness runners were rerun), harness tests 9/9; mobile Jest 109 suites / 2,249 tests, typecheck, strict unused locals/parameters, ESLint, Prettier PASS; `git diff --check` clean.
- DEVELOPMENT state and ACL verification (checked by the user in DEVELOPMENT; nothing below is claimed beyond what was physically checked):
  - applied: `20261104090000_clinical_amendments.sql`;
  - `appointment_clinical_record_accessible(uuid)` (private shared access predicate): SECURITY DEFINER true, STABLE, `search_path=""`, `anon` EXECUTE false, `authenticated` EXECUTE false, `service_role` EXECUTE false;
  - `create_appointment_clinical_amendment(uuid,uuid,uuid,uuid,text,text)` (client wrapper): SECURITY DEFINER true, VOLATILE, `search_path=""`, `anon` false, `authenticated` true, `service_role` false;
  - `create_clinical_amendment(uuid,text,text,uuid)` (legacy/trusted writer): SECURITY DEFINER true, VOLATILE, `search_path=""`, `anon` false, `authenticated` false, `service_role` true — no longer a client path;

### Professional Longitudinal / Cross-Clinic Medical History + Veterinarian Provenance — Phase 1 (SPEC-MED-005 §15/§16, SPEC-ID-006 BR-41)

- Status: **Professional Longitudinal / Cross-Clinic Medical History + Veterinarian Provenance — Phase 1: CLOSED in DEVELOPMENT.**
  - Implemented and fully validated locally; the user applied `20261105090000_professional_longitudinal_history.sql` to DEVELOPMENT, physically checked the ACLs there, and physically validated the key Professional history workflow on Android (details in the DEVELOPMENT closure below).
  - Appointment-keyed audited Professional history applied and physically validated; the Pet's complete finalized history across Clinics and Organizations available under the specified care window; meaningful historical Veterinarian provenance delivered; internal identity ids removed from client payloads; Guardian history hardened without expanding Veterinarian-name visibility; Appointment detail stays audit-neutral until the explicit history action; no Appointment Veterinarian Assignment or Veterinarian Availability dependency.
  - **Production has not been deployed or touched.** `20261105090000_professional_longitudinal_history.sql` remains pending there.

- Small-clinic first: a solo Veterinarian with a confirmed or requested Appointment taps **Ver historial clínico** and reads the Pet's history; no Assignment, Availability, routing or approval step exists.
- Specs: SPEC-MED-005 §15 (BR-38–BR-47 + mobile presentation) and §16 (Notes 29–39); SPEC-ID-006 BR-41 (professional medical-history provenance visibility context: display name only, historical attribution, no current Membership, no email/Invitation address/credential/identifier/title, not for Guardians) and Note 23; SPEC-MED-009 §4 and SPEC-MED-004 §15 cross-references (the D2 Amendment basis is unchanged); REQUIREMENTS REQ-ACC-003 traceability; RELATIONAL_MODEL client-path row (no schema change).
- Migration `20261105090000_professional_longitudinal_history.sql` (no table, column, constraint or trigger change):

  - `view_appointment_professional_medical_history(p_clinic_id, p_appointment_id, p_limit=50, p_before_history_order_at, p_before_entry_id)`: SECURITY DEFINER, VOLATILE, `search_path=""`, authenticated only (`public`, `anon`, `service_role` revoked). Order: authenticate (`28000`) → argument shape (`22023` `INVALID_LIMIT` / `INVALID_CURSOR`) → current exact-Clinic Veterinarian context with `CLINICAL_CARE`, share-locked (Membership, Grant, Clinic Assignment) and re-decided, plus the `CLINICAL_CARE` Entitlement → the Appointment of that Clinic → its Care Authorization and Encounter (share-locked) → the window. Every refusal from the context on is exactly `{"status":"DENIED"}`. `{"status":"PERMITTED","entries":[…],"cursor":…}`; each Entry carries only `entry_id`, category, content, clinical/history/finalization/Encounter-start times, Clinic and Organization names, `is_this_appointment`, `author` / `finalizer` (`{display_name, is_me}`, finalizer `null` when the author finalized), Amendments (`amendment_id`, reason, content, `created_at`, `author`), `amendment_count`, `amendments_truncated`. One `VIEW_PET_MEDICAL_HISTORY` audit event per permitted page (current access context: Care Authorization, Clinic, Organization, Appointment, Membership, Grant, Clinic Assignment, Encounter where present); a refusal of an Appointment of the named Clinic is audited `DENIED`; an unknown or foreign Appointment establishes no Pet and is not audited. Keyset page 50, max 100;

The superseded private chain (`slice12_view_guardian_pet_medical_history`, `slice13_view_guardian_pet_medical_history`, `decorate_guardian_medical_history_provenance_names`) is dropped;
  - authenticated `EXECUTE` withdrawn from the legacy `view_professional_pet_medical_history` (client-supplied Pet/Care Authorization/Encounter ids, internal ids); it remains a trusted internal function (no mobile client ever called it).
- Exact access matrix (SPEC-MED-005 BR-40), enforced by the private predicate: `REQUESTED` future start, no Encounter → permitted; `REQUESTED` start passed → denied; `CONFIRMED`, no Encounter → permitted; `CONFIRMED` + `ACTIVE` → permitted; `CONFIRMED` + `CONCLUDED` → denied; `COMPLETED`, no Encounter → denied; `COMPLETED` + `ACTIVE` (retrospective) → permitted; `COMPLETED` + `CONCLUDED` → denied; `REVOKED` or `ENDED` (also during an `ACTIVE` Encounter) → denied; `CANCELLED` / `NO_SHOW` / `EXPIRED` → denied; Entitlement lapse → denied, also during an `ACTIVE` Encounter (the Encounter's own SPEC-MED-009 record keeps its continuity). Known unresolved policy preserved, not solved: an unresolved past `CONFIRMED` Appointment keeps permitting pre-care history while its Care Authorization stays `ACTIVE` (non-blocking).
- Offboarded authors: the name is resolved through the historically recorded User, never the current Membership, so an author whose Membership, `VETERINARIAN` Grant or Clinic Assignment ended keeps a meaningful name; a renamed User shows the current name; a blank or missing name shows the neutral fallback.
- Mobile: new route `[appointmentId]/history` (**Historial clínico / Medical history**) — reads once on arrival (the tap is the explicit action), never on focus; Retry and Load more re-read; latest request wins and a Clinic/Appointment change starts from nothing; a denial on any page drops every clinical content for a calm unavailable state; flat timeline; collapsed cards read date and category → Clinic · Organization → Veterinarian (with "(tú)" for the reader) → **N correcciones**; expanded: original content first, finalization time, finalizer when different, corrections oldest first with reason, content, time and **Corrección por …**; no Amendment action, no attachments. Entry points: the Appointment detail's **Ver historial clínico** (advisory: `CLINICAL_CARE`, no Encounter, `CONFIRMED` or `REQUESTED` with a future start; opening the detail reads nothing) and the Clinical record while its Encounter is `ACTIVE` and the reader holds `CLINICAL_CARE` (`may_conclude`); hidden for `COMPLETED` without Encounter and after `CONCLUDED`. Guardian mobile types drop the removed ids (`clinicalEncounterId`, `clinicId`, `encounterLifecycleState`, `amendingUserId`, `amendingVeterinarianClinicAssignmentId`); Guardian presentation unchanged. ES/EN copy. No dependency, no native change, no new build.
